=== PART 2 (2026-10-18, controller J5) — restart, defect found, fix, re-verified ===

Defect found on restart: batch totals were non-idempotent under chunk redelivery.
The first worker kill had landed mid-shard-2 (BullMQ job ats=1, no finishedOn). On
restart BullMQ redelivered the chunk; the old code did `total = total + rows` per
attempt, so shard-2's 290 rows were counted twice: after all 3 chunks completed,
total=1196 while done+failed+skipped=128+0+778=906. The completion check
`done+failed+skipped >= total` never fired and the batch stayed RUNNING forever
(BullMQ job hashes show all three chunks completed: shard-1 {38,0,298},
shard-2 {43,0,247}, shard-3 {47,0,233}).

Fix (commit 290e156): chunks write per-(batch, shard) rows to payroll_batch_shards
(migration 020) and batch totals/status are recomputed from those rows; redelivery
rewrites its own row. Completion also requires all ACTIVE_SHARDS to have reported.
Regression test: apps/api/test/payroll-retry.test.ts (2 consecutive focused runs
green, logs payroll-focused-{3,4}.log).

-- recovery of the stuck batch (105260250617106432) after the fix --
Re-armed its 3 chunks (same payload; fresh jobIds) the way BullMQ stall redelivery
would (ops script payroll-rearm.mjs): worker completed all 3; poll (new HR session):
{"batch_id":"105260250617106432","period":"2026-10","status":"COMPLETED","total":906,"done":128,"failed":0,"skipped":778,"created_by":"aaaaaaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa","created_at":"2026-10-18T11:06:39.391Z"}
Now total = done + skipped exactly (906 = 128 + 778).

-- cycle 2: fresh kill on the fixed build (native redelivery, no manual re-arm) --
Batch: 105288732818567168 (period 2026-10). Kill: listener on :4001 tree-killed ~1s
after POST (npm error "tsx src/index.ts failed" in worker log; BullMQ job shard-2
left ACTIVE: ats=1, no finishedOn; shard-1 completed pre-kill).

While worker down (3 polls, 4s apart; docs/experiments/raw via scenario-e2-down-polls.txt):
poll 1: {"batch_id":"105288732818567168","period":"2026-10","status":"RUNNING","total":336,"done":38,"failed":0,"skipped":298}
poll 2: {"batch_id":"105288732818567168","period":"2026-10","status":"RUNNING","total":336,"done":38,"failed":0,"skipped":298}
poll 3: {"batch_id":"105288732818567168","period":"2026-10","status":"RUNNING","total":336,"done":38,"failed":0,"skipped":298}
=> not falsely complete; only shard-1's row counted (336 = 38 + 298).

Worker restarted (npm --workspace @hrms/worker run dev). Poll (4s apart):
poll 1: RUNNING  total 616 done 85  skipped 531   (shard-3 delivered; 616 = 336+280)
